Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the sorting structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 475. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 825 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to sorting theory, we decompose the problem into 475 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 825. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
